   1++6ED8              ; ============================================================================
   2++6ED8              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6ED8              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6ED8              ;
   5++6ED8              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6ED8              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6ED8              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6ED8              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6ED8              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6ED8              ; ============================================================================
  11++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6ED8              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6ED8
  18++6ED8              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6ED8                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6ED8 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6EDB 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6EDE B7                   or      a
  23++6EDF ED 52                sbc     hl,de

  25++6EE4

  27++6EE4 21 8B 94             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6EE7 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry

  43++6F08 CA 0E 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok

  45++6F0E              p127_layout_end_ok:

   1++6F74              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F74              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6F74              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6F74              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6F74              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6F74              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6F74              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6F74              ; ============================================================================
   9++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6F74
  13++6F74              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6F74              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6F74              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6F74              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6F74              p127_resident_start:
  18++6F74
  19++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6F74              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6F74
  26++6F74              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6F74              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6F74              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6F74              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6F74              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6F74 32 FE 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6F77 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6F78 32 FF 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6F7B 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6F7C 32 00 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6F7F 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6F80 32 01 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6F83
  39++6F83 3A FE 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6F86 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6F88 C2 B2 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6F8B 3A FF 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6F8E FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6F90 C2 B2 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6F93 3A 00 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6F96 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6F98 C2 B2 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6F9B 3A 01 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6F9E E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6FA0 CA B2 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6FA3                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6FA3 3A FE 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6FA6 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6FA8 CA B2 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6FAB FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6FAD CA B2 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6FB0 B7                   or      a
  58++6FB1 C9                   ret
  59++6FB2
  60++6FB2              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6FB2 37                   scf
  62++6FB3 C9                   ret
  63++6FB4
  64++6FB4              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6FB4              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6FB4              p127_resident_selftest:

  70++6FBA 16 01                ld      d,1

  72++6FBF DA FC 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail

 348++71A5              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++71A5              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++71A5              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++71A5              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++71A5              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++71A5              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++71A5              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++71A5              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++71A5              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:
 357++71A5 F3                   di
 358++71A6                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++71A6 21 74 6F             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++71A9 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++71AC B7                   or      a
 362++71AD ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++71AF DA F2 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++71B2
 365++71B2 21 F4 71             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++71B5 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++71B8 B7                   or      a
 368++71B9 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++71BB D2 F2 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++71BE
 371++71BE                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++71BE 21 9F 71             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 373++71C1 11 74 6F             ld      de,p127_resident_start
 374++71C4 B7                   or      a
 375++71C5 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++71C7 DA F2 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++71CA
 378++71CA 21 9F 71             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 379++71CD 11 F4 71             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++71D0 B7                   or      a
 381++71D1 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++71D3 D2 F2 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++71D6
 384++71D6                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++71D6                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++71D6 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 387++71D9 39                   add     hl,sp
 388++71DA 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h
 389++71DD B7                   or      a

 396++71EA B7                   or      a

   1++71F4              ; ============================================================================
   2++71F4              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++71F4              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++71F4              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++71F4              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++71F4              ; ============================================================================
   7++71F4              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++71F4              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++71F4              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++71F4              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++71F4              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++71F4              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++71F4
  14++71F4              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++71F4              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++71F4              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++71F4              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++71F4              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++71F4              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++71F4              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++71F4              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++71F4              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++71F4              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++71F4              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7

 198++7336              ; Stage 2.17 — install a deterministic ATM710 IM2 vector while masked.
 199++7336              ; unreal.ini selects FloatBus=0 and FloatDOS=0, so TMainZ80::IntVec()
 200++7336              ; supplies #FF in normal and TR-DOS operation. This routine installs only
 201++7336              ; that vector slot, loads I=#B0 and selects IM2. It never executes EI.

   2++78B3              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++78B3              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++78B3              ; ABI:
   5++78B3              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++78B3              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++78B3              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++78B3              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++78B3              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++78B3              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.

   1++79CD              ; ============================================================================
   2++79CD              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++79CD              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++79CD              ;
   5++79CD              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++79CD              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++79CD              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++79CD              ;
   9++79CD              ; API:
  10++79CD              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++79CD              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++79CD              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++79CD              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++79CD              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++79CD              ;
  16++79CD              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++79CD              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++79CD              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++79CD              ; ============================================================================
  20++79CD              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++79CD              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++79CD              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++79CD              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++79CD              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++79CD
  26++79CD              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++79CD              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++79CD              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++79CD              p127_arena_init:
  30++79CD 21 72 78             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++79D0 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++79D2 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE

  35++79D5 B7                   or      a
  36++79D6 C2 E0 79             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++79D9 2B                   dec     hl
  38++79DA 0D                   dec     c
  39++79DB 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++79DD C3 93 7A             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++79E0              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++79E0 0C                   inc     c
  43++79E1 79                   ld      a,c
  44++79E2 FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++79E4 D2 93 7A             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++79E7 32 24 7B             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++79EA 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++79EC 91                   sub     c
  49++79ED CA 93 7A             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++79F0 32 25 7B             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++79F3 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++79F5 32 26 7B             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++79F8 B7                   or      a
  54++79F9 C9                   ret
  55++79FA
  56++79FA              ; IN B=non-zero page count. OUT A=first page of the new block.
  57++79FA              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.
  58++79FA              p127_arena_alloc:
  59++79FA 78                   ld      a,b
  60++79FB B7                   or      a
  61++79FC CA 93 7A             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  62++79FF 3A 26 7B             ld      a,(p127_arena_next_page)
  63++7A02 90                   sub     b
  64++7A03 DA 93 7A             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  65++7A06 32 27 7B             ld      (p127_arena_first_page),a
  66++7A09 4F                   ld      c,a
  67++7A0A 3A 24 7B             ld      a,(p127_arena_low_page)

  69++7A0E 79                   ld      a,c
  70++7A0F 93                   sub     e
  71++7A10 DA 93 7A             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  72++7A13 48                   ld      c,b
  73++7A14 3A 27 7B             ld      a,(p127_arena_first_page)
  74++7A17 5F                   ld      e,a
  75++7A18 16 00                ld      d,0
  76++7A1A 21 33 78             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  77++7A1D 19                   add     hl,de

   1++7B2B              ; ============================================================================
   2++7B2B              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7B2B              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7B2B              ;
   5++7B2B              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7B2B              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7B2B              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7B2B              ;
   9++7B2B              ; API:
  10++7B2B              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7B2B              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7B2B              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7B2B              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7B2B              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7B2B              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7B2B              ; ============================================================================
  17++7B2B              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7B2B              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7B2B              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++7B2B              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++7B2B              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++7B2B              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++7B2B
  24++7B2B              p127_as_init:
  25++7B2B 21 CC 7C             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++7B2E 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++7B30 11 CD 7C             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++7B33 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++7B36 ED B0                ldir

  31++7B39 C9                   ret
  32++7B3A
  33++7B3A              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.
  34++7B3A              p127_as_bind:

  36++7B3D 78                   ld      a,b

  38++7B40 D2 DE 7B             jp      nc,p127_as_reject

  49++7B56 5F                   ld      e,a
  50++7B57 16 00                ld      d,0

  59++7B68              .p127_as_bind_scan:

  82++7B88              p127_as_unbind:

  95++7B99              ; Page index = address >> 14; offset = address & 3FFFh.

   1++7FAC              ; ============================================================================
   2++7FAC              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7FAC              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7FAC              ;
   5++7FAC              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7FAC              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7FAC              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7FAC              ;
   9++7FAC              ; API inputs:
  10++7FAC              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7FAC              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7FAC              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7FAC              ; Outputs:
  14++7FAC              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7FAC              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7FAC              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7FAC              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7FAC              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7FAC              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7FAC              ; ============================================================================
  21++7FAC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7FAC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7FAC              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7FAC              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7FAC              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h

   1++8850              ; ============================================================================
   2++8850              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++8850              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8850              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++8850              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++8850              ; ============================================================================
   7++8850              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++8850              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++8850              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++8850
  11++8850              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++8850              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++8850              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++8850              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++8850              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++8850              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++8850              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++8850
  19++8850              ; IN: none.
  20++8850              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++8850              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++8850              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++8850              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++8850              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++8850              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++8850              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++8850 F3                   di
  28++8851 F5                   push    af
  29++8852 C5                   push    bc
  30++8853 D5                   push    de
  31++8854 E5                   push    hl
  32++8855 DD E5                push    ix
  33++8857 FD E5                push    iy
  34++8859 ED 73 E8 88          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++885D
  36++885D                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++885D 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++8860 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++8861 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++8864 B7                   or      a
  41++8865 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++8867 DA B8 88             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse

  45++886A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h

  47++886F 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah

   1++88EB              ; ============================================================================
   2++88EB              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++88EB              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++88EB              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++88EB              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++88EB              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++88EB              ; ============================================================================

   9++88EB              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

  12++88EB              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++88EB              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++88EB              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++88EB              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h
  16++88EB              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_COUNT EQU 128
  17++88EB              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  18++88EB

  20++88EB              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++88EB              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++88EB              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

 472+ 8E38                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_speaker.asm"
# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8E38              ; ============================================================================
   2++8E38              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8E38              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8E38              ;
   5++8E38              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8E38              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8E38              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8E38              ;
   9++8E38              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8E38              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8E38              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8E38              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8E38              ;
  14++8E38              ; API:
  15++8E38              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8E38              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8E38              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8E38              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8E38              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8E38              ;
  21++8E38              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8E38              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8E38              ; is assumed.
  24++8E38              ; ============================================================================
  25++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8E38
  29++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8E38              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8E38
  33++8E38              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8E38 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8E3A 32 96 8E             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8E3D C9                   ret
  37++8E3E
  38++8E3E              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8E3E              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8E3E              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8E3E              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8E3E C5                   push    bc
  43++8E3F 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8E40 3A 96 8E             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8E43 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8E45 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8E46 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8E47 E6 01                and     1
  49++8E49 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8E4B 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8E4C F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8E4E 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8E50              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8E50 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8E51              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8E51 32 96 8E             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8E54 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8E56 C1                   pop     bc
  59++8E57 C9                   ret
  60++8E58
  61++8E58              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8E58              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8E58 3A 96 8E             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8E5B EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8E5D 32 96 8E             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8E60 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8E62 C9                   ret
  68++8E63
  69++8E63              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8E63              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8E63 3A 96 8E             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8E66 E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8E68 32 96 8E             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8E6B D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8E6D C9                   ret
  76++8E6E
  77++8E6E              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8E6E 3A 96 8E             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8E71 C9                   ret
  80++8E72
  81++8E72
  82++8E72              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8E72              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8E72              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8E72              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8E72              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8E72 F5                   push    af
  88++8E73 C5                   push    bc
  89++8E74 D5                   push    de
  90++8E75 E5                   push    hl
  91++8E76 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8E78 CD 38 8E             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8E7B 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8E7E              p127_speaker_test_loop:

   1++916D              ; ============================================================================
   2++916D              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader
   3++916D              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++916D              ; Derived from Project127/113 FAT16 BPB/root-directory flow.
   5++916D              ; Uses the existing NEMO-DIVIDE LBA28 single-sector transport in p127_hdd.asm.
   6++916D              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.
   7++916D              ; Output: each root entry is mirrored to the ATM console and debug port #EF.
   8++916D              ; ============================================================================
   9++916D              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++916D              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  11++916D              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++916D
  13++916D              ; Parse the FAT16 BPB at LBA 63, calculate the absolute root-directory LBA,
  14++916D              ; read every root-directory sector and print each valid 8.3 entry.
  15++916D              ; IN: P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER contains the partition boot sector from probe.
  16++916D              ; OUT: carry=1 on invalid BPB or sector-read failure.
  17++916D              p127_hdd_list_root:
  18++916D F5                   push    af
  19++916E C5                   push    bc
  20++916F D5                   push    de
  21++9170 E5                   push    hl

  27++9179 32 77 94             ld      (p127_hdd_fat_file_count),a
  28++917C 32 78 94             ld      (p127_hdd_fat_file_count+1),a
  29++917F 21 B5 93             ld      hl,p127_hdd_fat_msg_begin
  30++9182 CD 12 63             call    p127_puts
  31++9185

# file opened: p127_console.asm
   1+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A000              ; Project127 ATM-native debug console, ported from Project126
   3+ A000              ; Source: Project126/atm_host/p126_screen_console.asm
   4+ A000              ; Module version: 0.1.4
   5+ A000              ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG6 80x25; upper 12 rows status, lower 13 rows scrolling log.
   6+ A000              ; Every displayed debug character and static UI label is mirrored to #EF.
   7+ A000              ; Resident placement: #A000, in the Project126 host display window.
   8+ A000              ; ============================================================================
   9+ A000              P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25 EQU 6
  10+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 80
  11+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS EQU 13
  12+ A000              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP EQU 12
  13+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE7 EQU #78
  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25

  22+ A000 06           p126_atm_video_mode: db P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0
  24+ A002 00           Console_X: db 0
  25+ A003 0C           Console_Y: db BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0
  28+ A006 00           bios_dbg_char: db 0
  29+ A007 00           bios_dbg_write_row: db 0
  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 263+ A163
 264+ A163 F5                   push    af
 265+ A164 C5                   push    bc
 266+ A165 D5                   push    de
 267+ A166 E5                   push    hl
 268+ A167 CD 12 70             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 313+ A1BA 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:
 316+ A1C0 CD 2D 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 318+ A1C6 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 319+ A1C8 ED 79                out     (c),a
 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af
 326+ A1D2 C9                   ret
 327+ A1D3              .p126_print_at_denied:
 328+ A1D3 E1                   pop     hl
 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de
 330+ A1D5 C1                   pop     bc
 331+ A1D6 F1                   pop     af
 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret

 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.
 335+ A1D8              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar:

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 644+ A43D D8                   ret     c
 645+ A43E 3A C3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_even)
 646+ A441 21 C7 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_even
 647+ A444 BE                   cp      (hl)
 648+ A445 20 2D                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 649+ A447 3A C4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_odd)
 650+ A44A 21 C8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_odd
 651+ A44D BE                   cp      (hl)
 652+ A44E 20 24                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 653+ A450 3A C5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_even)
 654+ A453 21 C9 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_even
 655+ A456 BE                   cp      (hl)
 656+ A457 20 1B                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 657+ A459 3A C6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_odd)
 658+ A45C 21 CA A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd
 659+ A45F BE                   cp      (hl)
 660+ A460 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 661+ A462                      ; Compare complete-page CRCs: P7 character plane and P3 attributes.
 662+ A462 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 663+ A465 11 DC A5             ld      de,p127_video_crc_after
 664+ A468 06 04                ld      b,4
 665+ A46A              p127_video_crc_compare_loop:
 666+ A46A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 667+ A46B BE                   cp      (hl)
 668+ A46C 20 06                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 669+ A46E 23                   inc     hl
 670+ A46F 13                   inc     de
 671+ A470 10 F8                djnz    p127_video_crc_compare_loop
 672+ A472 B7                   or      a
 673+ A473 C9                   ret
 674+ A474
 675+ A474              p127_video_test_fail:
 676+ A474 37                   scf
 677+ A475 C9                   ret
 678+ A476
 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.
 680+ A476              p127_video_switch_mode:
 681+ A476 32 C1 A5             ld      (p127_video_mode_arg),a
 682+ A479 C5                   push    bc
 683+ A47A D5                   push    de
 684+ A47B E5                   push    hl
 685+ A47C CD 12 70             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 686+ A47F 38 6A                jr      c,p127_video_switch_refused
 687+ A481 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 688+ A484
 689+ A484                      ; Preserve the original P3 bytes occupied by the temporary trampoline.
 690+ A484                      ; The trampoline returns to resident code, where these bytes are restored
 691+ A484                      ; before P3 is unmapped. This keeps the full-page CRC genuinely read-only.
 692+ A484 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 693+ A487 11 CB A5             ld      de,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 694+ A48A 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 695+ A48D ED B0                ldir
 696+ A48F
 697+ A48F                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p127_video_mode_return.
 698+ A48F 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 699+ A492 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 700+ A494 23                   inc     hl
 701+ A495 36 77                ld      (hl),#77
 702+ A497 23                   inc     hl
 703+ A498 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 704+ A49A 23                   inc     hl
 705+ A49B 36 ED                ld      (hl),#ED
 706+ A49D 23                   inc     hl
 707+ A49E 36 79                ld      (hl),#79
 708+ A4A0 23                   inc     hl
 709+ A4A1 36 C3                ld      (hl),#C3
 710+ A4A3 23                   inc     hl
 711+ A4A4 36 AF                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return & #FF
 712+ A4A6 23                   inc     hl
 713+ A4A7 36 A4                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return >> 8
 714+ A4A9 3A C1 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_mode_arg)
 715+ A4AC C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 716+ A4AF
 717+ A4AF              p127_video_mode_return:
 718+ A4AF                      ; P3 is still mapped here: restore the bytes overwritten by the trampoline.
 719+ A4AF 21 CB A5             ld      hl,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 720+ A4B2 11 EC FF             ld      de,#FFEC
 721+ A4B5 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 722+ A4B8 ED B0                ldir
 723+ A4BA CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 724+ A4BD CD 2D 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 725+ A4C0
 726+ A4C0                      ; Verify the CPU windows and W3 ownership contract after EVERY mode
 727+ A4C0                      ; write. W2 must be the host page P5, W3 must be P0, and the UI lease
 728+ A4C0                      ; must be released before another mode transition is attempted.
 729+ A4C0 3A 25 87             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+1)
 730+ A4C3 FE 05                cp      5
 731+ A4C5 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 732+ A4C7 3A 27 87             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 733+ A4CA B7                   or      a
 734+ A4CB 20 19                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 735+ A4CD 3A 9F 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 736+ A4D0 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 737+ A4D2 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 738+ A4D4 3A A0 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 739+ A4D7 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 740+ A4D9 20 0B                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 741+ A4DB 3A A1 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 742+ A4DE B7                   or      a
 743+ A4DF 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 744+ A4E1 E1                   pop     hl
 745+ A4E2 D1                   pop     de
 746+ A4E3 C1                   pop     bc
 747+ A4E4 B7                   or      a
 748+ A4E5 C9                   ret
 749+ A4E6
 750+ A4E6              p127_video_mode_restore_error:
 751+ A4E6 E1                   pop     hl
 752+ A4E7 D1                   pop     de
 753+ A4E8 C1                   pop     bc
 754+ A4E9 37                   scf
 755+ A4EA C9                   ret
 756+ A4EB
 757+ A4EB              p127_video_switch_refused:
 758+ A4EB E1                   pop     hl
 759+ A4EC D1                   pop     de
 760+ A4ED C1                   pop     bc
 761+ A4EE 37                   scf
 762+ A4EF C9                   ret
 763+ A4F0
 764+ A4F0              ; Capture bytes from both character parity planes and both attribute planes.
 765+ A4F0              ; OUT: before signature when p127_video_capture_target=0, after when =1.
 766+ A4F0              p127_video_capture_signature:

 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.

 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.

 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl

 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page

 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de

 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:

 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:

 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21
 868+ A59D 5F                   ld      e,a
 869+ A59E              p127_video_crc16_no_poly:
 870+ A59E 3A D3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_bits)
 871+ A5A1 3D                   dec     a
 872+ A5A2 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 873+ A5A5 20 E9                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_bit

 876+ A5A9 78                   ld      a,b
 877+ A5AA B1                   or      c
 878+ A5AB 20 DB                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_byte
 879+ A5AD C9                   ret

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

 229+ A7CF FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 230+ A7D1 30 2C                jr      nc,p127_physmem_alloc_complete
 231+ A7D3 CD D3 75             call    p127_mem_alloc
 232+ A7D6 38 27                jr      c,p127_physmem_alloc_complete
 233+ A7D8 32 5C AD             ld      (p127_physmem_candidate),a
 234+ A7DB 57                   ld      d,a
 235+ A7DC 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 236+ A7DF 47                   ld      b,a
 237+ A7E0 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 238+ A7E3              p127_physmem_duplicate_scan:
 239+ A7E3 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 240+ A7E4 BA                   cp      d
 241+ A7E5 CA B1 A8             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_duplicate
 242+ A7E8 23                   inc     hl
 243+ A7E9 10 F8                djnz    p127_physmem_duplicate_scan
 244+ A7EB 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 245+ A7EE 5F                   ld      e,a
 246+ A7EF 16 00                ld      d,0
 247+ A7F1 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list

 254+ A7FF
 255+ A7FF              p127_physmem_alloc_complete:
 256+ A7FF 3E 41                ld      a,'A'
 257+ A801 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace
 258+ A804                      ; Current map has seven protected pages and 57 allocatable.
 259+ A804 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 260+ A807 FE 39                cp      57
 261+ A809 C2 B6 A8             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_count
 262+ A80C CD 94 AA             call    p127_physmem_check_reserved
 263+ A80F DA BB A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_reserve
 264+ A812
 265+ A812                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A812                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.
 267+ A812 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 268+ A815 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 269+ A818 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 270+ A81B              p127_physmem_full_fill_loop:
 271+ A81B 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 272+ A81C 23                   inc     hl
 273+ A81D 32 5D AD             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 274+ A820 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 275+ A822 CD 94 AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_page
 276+ A825 3A 5D AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 277+ A828 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 278+ A82B DA A2 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 279+ A82E 3A 5D AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 280+ A831 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 281+ A833 57                   ld      d,a
 282+ A834 E5                   push    hl
 283+ A835 CD 28 AD             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern
 284+ A838 E1                   pop     hl
 285+ A839 3A 5E AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 286+ A83C 3D                   dec     a
 287+ A83D 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 288+ A840 20 D9                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_fill_loop
 289+ A842
 290+ A842 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 291+ A844 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace
 292+ A847
 293+ A847                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A847                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A847 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A84A 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A84B 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A84D 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A850 19                   add     hl,de
 300+ A851              p127_physmem_full_verify_loop:
 301+ A851 2B                   dec     hl
 302+ A852 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 303+ A853 32 5D AD             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 304+ A856 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 305+ A858 CD 94 AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_page
 306+ A85B 3A 5D AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 307+ A85E CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 308+ A861 DA A2 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 309+ A864 3A 5D AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 310+ A867 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 311+ A869 57                   ld      d,a
 312+ A86A E5                   push    hl
 313+ A86B CD 3A AD             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 314+ A86E E1                   pop     hl
 315+ A86F DA AC A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 316+ A872 3A 5E AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A875                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A875 B7                   or      a
 319+ A876 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A878 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A87B              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:
 322+ A87B 3D                   dec     a
 323+ A87C 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 324+ A87F 20 D0                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_verify_loop
 325+ A881
 326+ A881 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 327+ A883 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace
 328+ A886
 329+ A886                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.
 330+ A886 CD D5 AA             call    p127_physmem_check_allocated
 331+ A889 DA C0 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_state
 332+ A88C AF                   xor     a

 335+ A893
 336+ A893              p127_physmem_fail_map_state:
 337+ A893 3E 01                ld      a,1
 338+ A895 C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error

 347+ A8A4 C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 348+ A8A7              p127_physmem_fail_probe:

 350+ A8A9 C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 351+ A8AC              p127_physmem_fail_data:

 353+ A8AE C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 354+ A8B1              p127_physmem_fail_duplicate:

 356+ A8B3 C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 357+ A8B6              p127_physmem_fail_count:

 360+ A8BB              p127_physmem_fail_reserve:

 368+ A8C5              p127_physmem_cleanup:
 369+ A8C5 3E 43                ld      a,'C'
 370+ A8C7 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace
 371+ A8CA                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A8CA 3A 55 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 374+ A8CE 28 07                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_saved
 375+ A8D0 AF                   xor     a

 384+ A8E3
 385+ A8E3              p127_physmem_cleanup_release:

 391+ A8EF              p127_physmem_cleanup_resume:
 392+ A8EF 3A 53 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_quiesced)
 393+ A8F2 B7                   or      a

 397+ A8FB
 398+ A8FB              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:

 400+ A8FE 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 401+ A901 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 402+ A904 5F                   ld      e,a

 414+ A917 E1                   pop     hl
 415+ A918 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 416+ A91B 3A 5E AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 417+ A91E 3D                   dec     a
 418+ A91F 32 5E AD             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 419+ A922 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A924              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A924                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A924 3A 51 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 423+ A927 B7                   or      a
 424+ A928 20 21                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return

 430+ A935 C2 8D AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_error

 432+ A939 CD E9 75             call    p127_mem_free
 433+ A93C DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 434+ A93F CD 94 AA             call    p127_physmem_check_reserved
 435+ A942 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 436+ A945 CD F4 AA             call    p127_physmem_check_free_state
 437+ A948 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A94B              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:
 439+ A94B 3A 51 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 440+ A94E B7                   or      a
 441+ A94F 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A951                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A951                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A951                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A951 CD 58 A9             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log
 446+ A954 B7                   or      a
 447+ A955 C9                   ret
 448+ A956              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A956 37                   scf
 450+ A957 C9                   ret
 451+ A958
 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A958              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A958              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A958                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A958                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A958                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A958 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 461+ A95B                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A95B                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.

 578+ AA94              p127_physmem_check_reserved:

 592+ AAAC FE 02                cp      2
